dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003e\u003ca href=\"https://argent.swmansion.com\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003eArgent\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/strong\u003e is an \u003cstrong\u003eagentic web apps. Ask it to tap a button, run a profiler or reproduce an issue manually - all from within your CLI, without switching context.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" dir=\"auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"npx @swmansion/argent@latest init\n# or, in a pnpm project (where npm's devEngines check may refuse to run npx):\npnpm dlx @swmansion/argent@latest init\"\u003e\u003cpre\u003enpx @swmansion/argent@latest init\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e or, in a pnpm project (where npm's devEngines check may refuse to run npx):\u003c/span\u003e\npnpm dlx @swmansion/argent@latest init\u003c/pre\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eSupported platforms\u003c/h2\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-supported-platforms\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: Supported platforms\" href=\"#supported-platforms\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eArgent drives a growing set of targets through a single toolkit, each with the right interaction model - touch, remote or mouse:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cmarkdown-accessiblity-table\u003e\u003ctable\u003e\n\u003cthead\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003cth\u003ePlatform\u003c/th\u003e\n\u003cth\u003eTargets\u003c/th\u003e\n\u003cth\u003eInteraction\u003c/th\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003c/thead\u003e\n\u003ctbody\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eiOS\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eSimulators\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eTouch / gesture\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eAndroid\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eEmulators (AVDs) and physical devices over adb\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eTouch / gesture\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eTV\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eApple TV (tvOS), Android TV / Google TV, Amazon Fire TV (Vega)\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eD-pad / remote\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eDesktop \u0026amp; web\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eElectron and Chromium apps (incl. React Native Web / Expo web) over CDP\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eMouse / keyboard\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003c/tbody\u003e\n\u003c/table\u003e\u003c/markdown-accessiblity-table\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eCapabilities\u003c/h2\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-capabilities\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: Capabilities\" href=\"#capabilities\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eAutonomous mobile, TV and desktop development\u003c/strong\u003e - Allow your agent to work with iOS, Android, TV and Electron/web apps on its own - let it build, open, interact with the app and debug it. Ask for reproducing issues, testing features manually, profiling your app and much more, without ever interrupting your work.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eUI interaction\u003c/strong\u003e - Give your agent the full control toolkit - tapping, swiping, pinching, typing, gestures and hardware buttons on mobile; the directional remote on TV; mouse, scroll and drag on desktop/web. Let it navigate your app exactly as a user would, without lifting a finger.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eRecord \u0026amp; replay flows\u003c/strong\u003e - Capture a sequence of interactions once and let your agent replay it deterministically, so manual repros and smoke tests become repeatable.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eVisual regression\u003c/strong\u003e - Diff two screenshots (or a saved baseline against a live capture) with OCR- and font-aware comparison to catch unintended UI changes.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eProfiling with batteries included\u003c/strong\u003e - Argent can perform and analyze React Native (Hermes), React DevTools and native (Xcode Instruments / Android Perfetto) profiling sessions - down to fiber renders, CPU hotspots and cross-correlated commit-vs-hang reports. Get comprehensive summaries and ask to optimise your app where you find fit.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eDebugging and diagnostics\u003c/strong\u003e - Let your agent inspect logs, capture network traffic (JS \u003ccode\u003efetch\u003c/code\u003e and native), evaluate JS in the running app, walk the native UIKit and React component trees, and reproduce failing states - so you can jump straight to the fix.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eDesktop \u0026amp; web control\u003c/strong\u003e - For Electron and Chromium apps your agent can drive tabs, read and write cookies and storage, walk the DOM and inspect network over the Chrome DevTools Protocol.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eReact Native out of the box\u003c/strong\u003e - Argent works with React Native apps natively, so your agent can build, launch, and iterate on your RN project the same way it would any native app - no extra setup required.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cblockquote\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eTip:\u003c/strong\u003e Once installed, ask your assistant \u003cem\u003e\"What can Argent do?\"\u003c/em\u003e - it will walk you through all capabilities available.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003c/blockquote\u003e\n\u003cbr\u003e\n\u003cp align=\"center\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n  \u003ca dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch4 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003ePrerequisites\u003c/h4\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-prerequisites\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: Prerequisites\" href=\"#prerequisites\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eNode.js 20.12\u003c/strong\u003e or later\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eFor iOS / tvOS: macOS with \u003cstrong\u003eXcode\u003c/strong\u003e installed (Apple TV uses tvOS simulators — Xcode downloads the tvOS runtime on demand)\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eFor Android / Android TV: \u003cstrong\u003eAndroid SDK Platform Tools\u003c/strong\u003e (\u003ccode\u003eadb\u003c/code\u003e) on \u003ccode\u003ePATH\u003c/code\u003e, and the \u003cstrong\u003eAndroid Emulator\u003c/strong\u003e package if you want to boot AVDs from Argent. Create AVDs via Android Studio or \u003ccode\u003eavdmanager\u003c/code\u003e.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eFor Fire TV (Vega): the \u003cstrong\u003eVega SDK\u003c/strong\u003e (\u003ccode\u003evega\u003c/code\u003e CLI) on \u003ccode\u003ePATH\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eFor Electron / Chromium: nothing extra to control an already-running app - just launch it with \u003ccode\u003e--remote-debugging-port\u003c/code\u003e, or let Argent spawn your Electron app for you\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch5 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eLinux host: extra prerequisites for Android emulators\u003c/h5\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-linux-host-extra-prerequisites-for-android-emulators\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: Linux host: extra prerequisites for Android emulators\" href=\"#linux-host-extra-prerequisites-for-android-emulators\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eArgent runs Android emulators on Linux but the default install can be slow if a few host-side knobs aren't right. Cover these once and the experience matches macOS:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eKVM access.\u003c/strong\u003e The emulator falls back to slow software emulation (TCG) without \u003ccode\u003e/dev/kvm\u003c/code\u003e. Make sure virtualization is enabled in BIOS/UEFI (\u003ccode\u003evmx\u003c/code\u003e for Intel, \u003ccode\u003esvm\u003c/code\u003e for AMD in \u003ccode\u003e/proc/cpuinfo\u003c/code\u003e) and that your user can read/write \u003ccode\u003e/dev/kvm\u003c/code\u003e — on most distros that means joining the \u003ccode\u003ekvm\u003c/code\u003e group:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" dir=\"auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"sudo usermod -aG kvm \u0026quot;$USER\u0026quot;\n# log out and back in so the new group takes effect\"\u003e\u003cpre\u003esudo usermod -aG kvm \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e\"\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-smi\"\u003e$USER\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e\"\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/span\u003e\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e log out and back in so the new group takes effect\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/pre\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eGPU mode (\u003ccode\u003e-gpu swiftshader\u003c/code\u003e on Linux, override available).\u003c/strong\u003e The Android emulator's Linux GPU story is messy: \u003ccode\u003e-gpu auto\u003c/code\u003e frequently resolves to lavapipe (slow software Vulkan via host libvulkan, ~10× cold-boot regression on flagship hardware), and \u003ccode\u003e-gpu host\u003c/code\u003e silently produces a corrupted or black emulator window on hosts with non-trivial GL stacks — dual-GPU / Optimus laptops, NVIDIA + Mesa coexistence via libglvnd, Wayland sessions on hybrid graphics, headless / containerized hosts. The failure mode is invisible to argent's framebuffer-based screenshot tool, so an agent reports success while the developer sees a black window.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eArgent picks \u003ccode\u003e-gpu swiftshader\u003c/code\u003e on Linux for universal compatibility: it sidesteps the host GL stack entirely and renders via the emulator's bundled SwiftShader. On modern multi-core machines this is indistinguishably smooth from hardware-accelerated \u003ccode\u003e-gpu host\u003c/code\u003e (and far faster than lavapipe).\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eOverride with the \u003ccode\u003eARGENT_EMULATOR_GPU_MODE\u003c/code\u003e env var if you've verified \u003ccode\u003e-gpu host\u003c/code\u003e works on your machine (typical single-GPU Mesa box with a healthy X session):\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" dir=\"auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"ARGENT_EMULATOR_GPU_MODE=host argent ...\"\u003e\u003cpre\u003eARGENT_EMULATOR_GPU_MODE=host argent ...\u003c/pre\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eArgent's boot-device preflight prints a warning if \u003ccode\u003e/dev/kvm\u003c/code\u003e isn't usable — the condition that causes a 10–50× TCG-vs-KVM slowdown.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eSystem image.\u003c/strong\u003e Prefer the \u003ccode\u003edefault\u003c/code\u003e or \u003ccode\u003egoogle_apis\u003c/code\u003e variants of \u003ccode\u003ex86_64\u003c/code\u003e system images for headless agent workflows; \u003ccode\u003egoogle_apis_playstore\u003c/code\u003e adds noticeable boot-time CPU churn from Play services. Always pick \u003ccode\u003ex86_64\u003c/code\u003e on Intel/AMD hosts — ARM images run via QEMU translation and are dramatically slower.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eAVD config.\u003c/strong\u003e AVDs created via \u003ccode\u003eavdmanager create avd\u003c/code\u003e default to \u003ccode\u003ehw.gpu.enabled=no\u003c/code\u003e. Argent overrides this with an explicit \u003ccode\u003e-gpu\u003c/code\u003e arg at launch (so the on-disk config doesn't need editing). For the smoothest experience under heavy native builds (gradle compilations alongside the AVD), bump the AVD's RAM and CPU count — edit \u003ccode\u003e~/.android/avd/\u0026lt;name\u0026gt;.avd/config.ini\u003c/code\u003e:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"snippet-clipboard-content notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"hw.ramSize = 8192\nhw.cpu.ncore = 6\nvm.heapSize = 512\"\u003e\u003cpre class=\"notranslate\"\u003e\u003ccode\u003ehw.ramSize = 8192\nhw.cpu.ncore = 6\nvm.heapSize = 512\n\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/pre\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eStock 2 GB / 4 vCPU AVDs can be CPU-starved into wedged-system_server states by a concurrent gradle/Kotlin compile.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eHeadless / CI mode (\u003ccode\u003eARGENT_EMULATOR_NO_WINDOW=1\u003c/code\u003e).\u003c/strong\u003e Argent shows the emulator window by default so a local developer can see the AVD UI. In a headless context — CI runner, container, or a Wayland-only session where the emulator's bundled Qt has no \u003ccode\u003ewayland\u003c/code\u003e platform plugin and SIGABRTs on the crash-consent dialog — opt out by exporting \u003ccode\u003eARGENT_EMULATOR_NO_WINDOW=1\u003c/code\u003e before starting the tool-server. This appends \u003ccode\u003e-no-window\u003c/code\u003e to the spawn args, selecting \u003ccode\u003eqemu-system-x86_64-headless\u003c/code\u003e which doesn't need a Qt window. Argent's screencap-based screenshot tool reads the in-memory framebuffer correctly without a visible window.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch4 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eRun \u003ccode\u003einit\u003c/code\u003e in your project\u003c/h4\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-run-init-in-your-project\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: Run init in your project\" href=\"#run-init-in-your-project\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eFrom your project class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e or, in a pnpm project (where npm's devEngines check may refuse to run npx):\u003c/span\u003e\npnpm dlx @swmansion/argent@latest init\u003c/pre\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eThis command triggers an installation wizard which:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eInstalls \u003ccode\u003e@swmansion/argent\u003c/code\u003e globally\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eDetects your editor and registers the MCP server\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eCopies skills, rules, and agent definitions into your workspace\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch4 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003ePrefer a team (committable install)\" href=\"#share-argent-with-your-team-committable-install\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eBy default Argent installs \u003cstrong\u003eglobally\u003c/strong\u003e. To version Argent \u003cem\u003ewith your repo\u003c/em\u003e so every\nteammate gets the same setup on \u003ccode\u003enpm install\u003c/code\u003e — no per-developer global install, no\n\u003ccode\u003eargent init\u003c/code\u003e — choose the local mode:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" dir=\"auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"npx @swmansion/argent@latest init --local\n# or, in a pnpm project:\npnpm dlx @swmansion/argent@latest init --local\"\u003e\u003cpre\u003enpx @swmansion/argent@latest init --local\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e or, in a pnpm project:\u003c/span\u003e\npnpm dlx @swmansion/argent@latest init --local\u003c/pre\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cblockquote\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eNote: in a freshly \u003ccode\u003epnpm init\u003c/code\u003e-ed project, \u003ccode\u003enpx\u003c/code\u003e itself may refuse to run\n(npm's \u003ccode\u003edevEngines\u003c/code\u003e check) — use the \u003ccode\u003epnpm dlx\u003c/code\u003e form there.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003c/blockquote\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eThis adds \u003ccode\u003e@swmansion/argent\u003c/code\u003e to your project's \u003ccode\u003edevDependencies\u003c/code\u003e and writes MCP\nconfigs that launch the project-local copy (\u003ccode\u003enode node_modules/@swmansion/argent/dist/cli.js mcp\u003c/code\u003e).\nCommit \u003ccode\u003epackage.json\u003c/code\u003e + your lockfile, the generated MCP config (\u003ccode\u003e.mcp.json\u003c/code\u003e,\n\u003ccode\u003e.cursor/mcp.json\u003c/code\u003e, …), \u003ccode\u003e.argent/install.json\u003c/code\u003e, and the skills/rules/agents files.\nTeammates then just run \u003ccode\u003enpm install\u003c/code\u003e.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003ePass \u003ccode\u003e--global\u003c/code\u003e to force the default mode in scripts; \u003ccode\u003e--local\u003c/code\u003e and \u003ccode\u003e--global\u003c/code\u003e are\nmutually exclusive. A non-interactive (\u003ccode\u003e--yes\u003c/code\u003e) run defaults to global unless the\nproject already opted into local mode (a committed \u003ccode\u003e.argent/install.json\u003c/code\u003e, or\n\u003ccode\u003e@swmansion/argent\u003c/code\u003e declared in the project's own \u003ccode\u003epackage.json\u003c/code\u003e).\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cblockquote\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eIn local mode the committed MCP config runs the project-local copy, so the bare\n\u003ccode\u003eargent\u003c/code\u003e command is \u003cstrong\u003enot\u003c/strong\u003e on teammates' \u003ccode\u003ePATH\u003c/code\u003e. Note that \u003ccode\u003enpm install\u003c/code\u003e builds\nArgent's native deps (\u003ccode\u003etree-sitter\u003c/code\u003e) on each machine — prebuilt for macOS, Linux\nx64, and Windows x64; other targets (Linux arm64, Windows arm) compile from source\nand need a C/C++ toolchain.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003c/blockquote\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch2 init\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eInstall and configure MCP in the current workspace (\u003ccode\u003e--global\u003c/code\u003e default, \u003ccode\u003e--local\u003c/code\u003e for a committable devDependency)\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003eargent install\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eAlias for \u003ccode\u003einit\u003c/code\u003e command\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003eargent update\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003ePull the latest version and refresh workspace configuration (acts on the present install — both when a global install and a project devDependency coexist; \u003ccode\u003e--global\u003c/code\u003e/\u003ccode\u003e--local\u003c/code\u003e select explicitly)\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003eargent uninstall\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eUnregister the MCP server and uninstall the package (\u003ccode\u003e--global\u003c/code\u003e/\u003ccode\u003e--local\u003c/code\u003e choose which install — and its configs — is removed; non-interactive runs never remove a coexisting global install)\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003eargent remove\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eAlias for \u003ccode\u003euninstall\u003c/code\u003e command\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003eargent mcp\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eStart MCP server instance, used internally by agent\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003eargent tools\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eList tools exposed by the tool-server (\u003ccode\u003edescribe \u0026lt;name\u0026gt;\u003c/code\u003e for details)\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003eargent run\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eInvoke a tool by name\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003eargent server\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eManage the shared tool-server: \u003ccode\u003estart\u003c/code\u003e / \u003ccode\u003estatus\u003c/code\u003e / \u003ccode\u003estop\u003c/code\u003e / \u003ccode\u003elogs\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003eargent lens\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eOpen Argent Lens bound to a fresh coding-agent session — Claude by default, \u003ccode\u003e--agent\u003c/code\u003e selects codex/gemini/opencode/cursor (macOS; behind the \u003ccode\u003eargent-lens\u003c/code\u003e flag — run \u003ccode\u003eargent enable argent-lens\u003c/code\u003e first)\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003eargent link\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eRoute client requests to a remote tool-server\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003eargent unlink\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eRemove the persisted remote tool-server link\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003eargent enable\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eEnable a predefined feature flag (\u003ccode\u003e--scope project\u003c/code\u003e for project-local)\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003eargent disable\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eDisable a feature flag (\u003ccode\u003e--scope project\u003c/code\u003e for project-local)\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003eargent flags\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eList available feature flags and their state\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003eargent telemetry\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eManage telemetry: \u003ccode\u003estatus\u003c/code\u003e / \u003ccode\u003eenable\u003c/code\u003e / \u003ccode\u003edisable\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003c/tbody\u003e\n\u003c/table\u003e\u003c/markdown-accessiblity-table\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" <p dir="auto"><a href="https://swm-delivery.com/www/delivery/ck-slug.php?zoneid=zone-gh-argent-1&amp;n=1" rel="nofollow"><img src="https://camo.githubusercontent.com/463890af1df225572e0fd98ee65ece56a55af62dea03ceb9546cbfbead9d07b6/68747470733a2f2f73776d2d64656c69766572792e636f6d2f7777772f696d616765732f7a6f6e652d67682d617267656e742d313f6e3d31" alt="Ad" data-canonical-src="https://swm-delivery.com/www/images/zone-gh-argent-1?n=1" style="max-width: 100%;"></a>
<a href="https://swm-delivery.com/www/delivery/ck-slug.php?zoneid=zone-gh-argent-2&amp;n=1" rel="nofollow"><img src="https://camo.githubusercontent.com/07542993424b3b78ac3c72f417e5103e828336c2c0291f1793b6d24ecec907cd/68747470733a2f2f73776d2d64656c69766572792e636f6d2f7777772f696d616765732f7a6f6e652d67682d617267656e742d323f6e3d31" alt="Ad" data-canonical-src="https://swm-delivery.com/www/images/zone-gh-argent-2?n=1" style="max-width: 100%;"></a>
<a href="https://swm-delivery.com/www/delivery/ck-slug.php?zoneid=zone-gh-argent-3&amp;n=1" rel="nofollow"><img src="https://camo.githubusercontent.com/64d645e20b2b31ddf039bfd0058f04b1380f0d415a6d79a00120ccde85dcacc5/68747470733a2f2f73776d2d64656c69766572792e636f6d2f7777772f696d616765732f7a6f6e652d67682d617267656e742d333f6e3d31" alt="Ad" data-canonical-src="https://swm-delivery.com/www/images/zone-gh-argent-3?n=1" style="max-width: 100%;"></a></p>
<p dir="auto"><strong><a href="https://argent.swmansion.com" rel="nofollow">Argent</a></strong> is an <strong>agentic toolkit</strong> that gives your AI assistant direct access to iOS Simulators, Android emulators and physical devices, TVs (Apple TV, Android TV, Fire TV) and Electron/Chromium desktop and web apps. Ask it to tap a button, run a profiler or reproduce an issue manually - all from within your CLI, without switching context.</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="npx @swmansion/argent@latest init
# or, in a pnpm project (where npm's devEngines check may refuse to run npx):
pnpm dlx @swmansion/argent@latest init"><pre>npx @swmansion/argent@latest init
<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> or, in a pnpm project (where npm's devEngines check may refuse to run npx):</span>
pnpm dlx @swmansion/argent@latest init</pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Supported platforms</h2><a id="user-content-supported-platforms" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Supported platforms" href="#supported-platforms"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Argent drives a growing set of targets through a single toolkit, each with the right interaction model - touch, remote or mouse:</p>
<markdown-accessiblity-table><table>
<thead>
<tr>
<th>Platform</th>
<th>Targets</th>
<th>Interaction</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td><strong>iOS</strong></td>
<td>Simulators</td>
<td>Touch / gesture</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><strong>Android</strong></td>
<td>Emulators (AVDs) and physical devices over adb</td>
<td>Touch / gesture</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><strong>TV</strong></td>
<td>Apple TV (tvOS), Android TV / Google TV, Amazon Fire TV (Vega)</td>
<td>D-pad / remote</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><strong>Desktop &amp; web</strong></td>
<td>Electron and Chromium apps (incl. React Native Web / Expo web) over CDP</td>
<td>Mouse / keyboard</td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></markdown-accessiblity-table>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Capabilities</h2><a id="user-content-capabilities" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Capabilities" href="#capabilities"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><strong>Autonomous mobile, TV and desktop development</strong> - Allow your agent to work with iOS, Android, TV and Electron/web apps on its own - let it build, open, interact with the app and debug it. Ask for reproducing issues, testing features manually, profiling your app and much more, without ever interrupting your work.</li>
<li><strong>UI interaction</strong> - Give your agent the full control toolkit - tapping, swiping, pinching, typing, gestures and hardware buttons on mobile; the directional remote on TV; mouse, scroll and drag on desktop/web. Let it navigate your app exactly as a user would, without lifting a finger.</li>
<li><strong>Record &amp; replay flows</strong> - Capture a sequence of interactions once and let your agent replay it deterministically, so manual repros and smoke tests become repeatable.</li>
<li><strong>Visual regression</strong> - Diff two screenshots (or a saved baseline against a live capture) with OCR- and font-aware comparison to catch unintended UI changes.</li>
<li><strong>Profiling with batteries included</strong> - Argent can perform and analyze React Native (Hermes), React DevTools and native (Xcode Instruments / Android Perfetto) profiling sessions - down to fiber renders, CPU hotspots and cross-correlated commit-vs-hang reports. Get comprehensive summaries and ask to optimise your app where you find fit.</li>
<li><strong>Debugging and diagnostics</strong> - Let your agent inspect logs, capture network traffic (JS <code>fetch</code> and native), evaluate JS in the running app, walk the native UIKit and React component trees, and reproduce failing states - so you can jump straight to the fix.</li>
<li><strong>Desktop &amp; web control</strong> - For Electron and Chromium apps your agent can drive tabs, read and write cookies and storage, walk the DOM and inspect network over the Chrome DevTools Protocol.</li>
<li><strong>React Native out of the box</strong> - Argent works with React Native apps natively, so your agent can build, launch, and iterate on your RN project the same way it would any native app - no extra setup required.</li>
</ul>
<blockquote>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Tip:</strong> Once installed, ask your assistant <em>"What can Argent do?"</em> - it will walk you through all capabilities available.</p>
</blockquote>
<br>
<p align="center" dir="auto">
  <a target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" href="https://github.com/software-mansion/argent/blob/main/assets/showcase.gif"><img src="https://github.com/software-mansion/argent/raw/main/assets/showcase.gif" alt="argent showcase video gif" width="100%" data-animated-image="" style="max-width: 100%;"></a>
</p>
<hr>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Installation</h2><a id="user-content-installation" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Installation" href="#installation"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h4 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Prerequisites</h4><a id="user-content-prerequisites" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Prerequisites" href="#prerequisites"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><strong>Node.js 20.12</strong> or later</li>
<li>For iOS / tvOS: macOS with <strong>Xcode</strong> installed (Apple TV uses tvOS simulators — Xcode downloads the tvOS runtime on demand)</li>
<li>For Android / Android TV: <strong>Android SDK Platform Tools</strong> (<code>adb</code>) on <code>PATH</code>, and the <strong>Android Emulator</strong> package if you want to boot AVDs from Argent. Create AVDs via Android Studio or <code>avdmanager</code>.</li>
<li>For Fire TV (Vega): the <strong>Vega SDK</strong> (<code>vega</code> CLI) on <code>PATH</code></li>
<li>For Electron / Chromium: nothing extra to control an already-running app - just launch it with <code>--remote-debugging-port</code>, or let Argent spawn your Electron app for you</li>
</ul>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h5 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Linux host: extra prerequisites for Android emulators</h5><a id="user-content-linux-host-extra-prerequisites-for-android-emulators" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Linux host: extra prerequisites for Android emulators" href="#linux-host-extra-prerequisites-for-android-emulators"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Argent runs Android emulators on Linux but the default install can be slow if a few host-side knobs aren't right. Cover these once and the experience matches macOS:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>
<p dir="auto"><strong>KVM access.</strong> The emulator falls back to slow software emulation (TCG) without <code>/dev/kvm</code>. Make sure virtualization is enabled in BIOS/UEFI (<code>vmx</code> for Intel, <code>svm</code> for AMD in <code>/proc/cpuinfo</code>) and that your user can read/write <code>/dev/kvm</code> — on most distros that means joining the <code>kvm</code> group:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="sudo usermod -aG kvm &quot;$USER&quot;
# log out and back in so the new group takes effect"><pre>sudo usermod -aG kvm <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span><span class="pl-smi">$USER</span><span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>
<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> log out and back in so the new group takes effect</span></pre></div>
</li>
<li>
<p dir="auto"><strong>GPU mode (<code>-gpu swiftshader</code> on Linux, override available).</strong> The Android emulator's Linux GPU story is messy: <code>-gpu auto</code> frequently resolves to lavapipe (slow software Vulkan via host libvulkan, ~10× cold-boot regression on flagship hardware), and <code>-gpu host</code> silently produces a corrupted or black emulator window on hosts with non-trivial GL stacks — dual-GPU / Optimus laptops, NVIDIA + Mesa coexistence via libglvnd, Wayland sessions on hybrid graphics, headless / containerized hosts. The failure mode is invisible to argent's framebuffer-based screenshot tool, so an agent reports success while the developer sees a black window.</p>
<p dir="auto">Argent picks <code>-gpu swiftshader</code> on Linux for universal compatibility: it sidesteps the host GL stack entirely and renders via the emulator's bundled SwiftShader. On modern multi-core machines this is indistinguishably smooth from hardware-accelerated <code>-gpu host</code> (and far faster than lavapipe).</p>
<p dir="auto">Override with the <code>ARGENT_EMULATOR_GPU_MODE</code> env var if you've verified <code>-gpu host</code> works on your machine (typical single-GPU Mesa box with a healthy X session):</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="ARGENT_EMULATOR_GPU_MODE=host argent ..."><pre>ARGENT_EMULATOR_GPU_MODE=host argent ...</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">Argent's boot-device preflight prints a warning if <code>/dev/kvm</code> isn't usable — the condition that causes a 10–50× TCG-vs-KVM slowdown.</p>
</li>
<li>
<p dir="auto"><strong>System image.</strong> Prefer the <code>default</code> or <code>google_apis</code> variants of <code>x86_64</code> system images for headless agent workflows; <code>google_apis_playstore</code> adds noticeable boot-time CPU churn from Play services. Always pick <code>x86_64</code> on Intel/AMD hosts — ARM images run via QEMU translation and are dramatically slower.</p>
</li>
<li>
<p dir="auto"><strong>AVD config.</strong> AVDs created via <code>avdmanager create avd</code> default to <code>hw.gpu.enabled=no</code>. Argent overrides this with an explicit <code>-gpu</code> arg at launch (so the on-disk config doesn't need editing). For the smoothest experience under heavy native builds (gradle compilations alongside the AVD), bump the AVD's RAM and CPU count — edit <code>~/.android/avd/&lt;name&gt;.avd/config.ini</code>:</p>
<div class="snippet-clipboard-content notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="hw.ramSize = 8192
hw.cpu.ncore = 6
vm.heapSize = 512"><pre class="notranslate"><code>hw.ramSize = 8192
hw.cpu.ncore = 6
vm.heapSize = 512
</code></pre></div>
<p dir="auto">Stock 2 GB / 4 vCPU AVDs can be CPU-starved into wedged-system_server states by a concurrent gradle/Kotlin compile.</p>
</li>
<li>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Headless / CI mode (<code>ARGENT_EMULATOR_NO_WINDOW=1</code>).</strong> Argent shows the emulator window by default so a local developer can see the AVD UI. In a headless context — CI runner, container, or a Wayland-only session where the emulator's bundled Qt has no <code>wayland</code> platform plugin and SIGABRTs on the crash-consent dialog — opt out by exporting <code>ARGENT_EMULATOR_NO_WINDOW=1</code> before starting the tool-server. This appends <code>-no-window</code> to the spawn args, selecting <code>qemu-system-x86_64-headless</code> which doesn't need a Qt window. Argent's screencap-based screenshot tool reads the in-memory framebuffer correctly without a visible window.</p>
</li>
</ul>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h4 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Run <code>init</code> in your project</h4><a id="user-content-run-init-in-your-project" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Run init in your project" href="#run-init-in-your-project"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">From your project root:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="npx @swmansion/argent@latest init
# or, in a pnpm project (where npm's devEngines check may refuse to run npx):
pnpm dlx @swmansion/argent@latest init"><pre>npx @swmansion/argent@latest init
<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> or, in a pnpm project (where npm's devEngines check may refuse to run npx):</span>
pnpm dlx @swmansion/argent@latest init</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">This command triggers an installation wizard which:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Installs <code>@swmansion/argent</code> globally</li>
<li>Detects your editor and registers the MCP server</li>
<li>Copies skills, rules, and agent definitions into your workspace</li>
</ul>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h4 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Prefer a manual install?</h4><a id="user-content-prefer-a-manual-install" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Prefer a manual install?" href="#prefer-a-manual-install"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="npm install -g @swmansion/argent
argent init"><pre>npm install -g @swmansion/argent
argent init</pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h4 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Share Argent with your team (committable install)</h4><a id="user-content-share-argent-with-your-team-committable-install" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Share Argent with your team (committable install)" href="#share-argent-with-your-team-committable-install"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">By default Argent installs <strong>globally</strong>. To version Argent <em>with your repo</em> so every
teammate gets the same setup on <code>npm install</code> — no per-developer global install, no
<code>argent init</code> — choose the local mode:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="npx @swmansion/argent@latest init --local
# or, in a pnpm project:
pnpm dlx @swmansion/argent@latest init --local"><pre>npx @swmansion/argent@latest init --local
<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> or, in a pnpm project:</span>
pnpm dlx @swmansion/argent@latest init --local</pre></div>
<blockquote>
<p dir="auto">Note: in a freshly <code>pnpm init</code>-ed project, <code>npx</code> itself may refuse to run
(npm's <code>devEngines</code> check) — use the <code>pnpm dlx</code> form there.</p>
</blockquote>
<p dir="auto">This adds <code>@swmansion/argent</code> to your project's <code>devDependencies</code> and writes MCP
configs that launch the project-local copy (<code>node node_modules/@swmansion/argent/dist/cli.js mcp</code>).
Commit <code>package.json</code> + your lockfile, the generated MCP config (<code>.mcp.json</code>,
<code>.cursor/mcp.json</code>, …), <code>.argent/install.json</code>, and the skills/rules/agents files.
Teammates then just run <code>npm install</code>.</p>
<p dir="auto">Pass <code>--global</code> to force the default mode in scripts; <code>--local</code> and <code>--global</code> are
mutually exclusive. A non-interactive (<code>--yes</code>) run defaults to global unless the
project already opted into local mode (a committed <code>.argent/install.json</code>, or
<code>@swmansion/argent</code> declared in the project's own <code>package.json</code>).</p>
<blockquote>
<p dir="auto">In local mode the committed MCP config runs the project-local copy, so the bare
<code>argent</code> command is <strong>not</strong> on teammates' <code>PATH</code>. Note that <code>npm install</code> builds
Argent's native deps (<code>tree-sitter</code>) on each machine — prebuilt for macOS, Linux
x64, and Windows x64; other targets (Linux arm64, Windows arm) compile from source
and need a C/C++ toolchain.</p>
</blockquote>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">CLI Reference</h2><a id="user-content-cli-reference" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: CLI Reference" href="#cli-reference"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<markdown-accessiblity-table><table>
<thead>
<tr>
<th>Command</th>
<th>Description</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td><code>argent init</code></td>
<td>Install and configure MCP in the current workspace (<code>--global</code> default, <code>--local</code> for a committable devDependency)</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>argent install</code></td>
<td>Alias for <code>init</code> command</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>argent update</code></td>
<td>Pull the latest version and refresh workspace configuration (acts on the present install — both when a global install and a project devDependency coexist; <code>--global</code>/<code>--local</code> select explicitly)</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>argent uninstall</code></td>
<td>Unregister the MCP server and uninstall the package (<code>--global</code>/<code>--local</code> choose which install — and its configs — is removed; non-interactive runs never remove a coexisting global install)</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>argent remove</code></td>
<td>Alias for <code>uninstall</code> command</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>argent mcp</code></td>
<td>Start MCP server instance, used internally by agent</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>argent tools</code></td>
<td>List tools exposed by the tool-server (<code>describe &lt;name&gt;</code> for details)</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>argent run</code></td>
<td>Invoke a tool by name</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>argent server</code></td>
<td>Manage the shared tool-server: <code>start</code> / <code>status</code> / <code>stop</code> / <code>logs</code></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>argent lens</code></td>
<td>Open Argent Lens bound to a fresh coding-agent session — Claude by default, <code>--agent</code> selects codex/gemini/opencode/cursor (macOS; behind the <code>argent-lens</code> flag — run <code>argent enable argent-lens</code> first)</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>argent link</code></td>
<td>Route client requests to a remote tool-server</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>argent unlink</code></td>
<td>Remove the persisted remote tool-server link</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>argent enable</code></td>
<td>Enable a predefined feature flag (<code>--scope project</code> for project-local)</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>argent disable</code></td>
<td>Disable a feature flag (<code>--scope project</code> for project-local)</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>argent flags</code></td>
<td>List available feature flags and their state</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>argent telemetry</code></td>
<td>Manage telemetry: <code>status</code> / <code>enable</code> / <code>disable</code></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></markdown-accessiblity-table>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Supported Editors</h2><a id="user-content-supported-editors" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Supported Editors" href="#supported-editors"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto"><code>argent init</code> auto-detects and configures MCP for:</p>
<markdown-accessiblity-table><table>
<thead>
<tr>
<th>Editor</th>
<th>Config location</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td>Claude Code</td>
<td><code>.mcp.json</code> (project) or <code>~/.claude.json</code> (global)</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>Cursor</td>
<td><code>.cursor/mcp.json</code> (project) or <code>~/.cursor/mcp.json</code> (global)</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>VS Code</td>
<td><code>.vscode/mcp.json</code></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>Windsurf</td>
<td><code>~/.codeium/windsurf/mcp_config.json</code> (global)</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>Zed</td>
<td><code>.zed/settings.json</code> (project) or <code>~/.config/zed/settings.json</code> (global)</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>Gemini CLI</td>
<td><code>.gemini/settings.json</code></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>Codex CLI</td>
<td><code>.codex/config.toml</code> (project) or <code>~/.codex/config.toml</code> (global)</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>Hermes</td>
<td><code>~/.hermes/config.yaml</code> (global)</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>opencode</td>
<td><code>opencode.json</code> (project) or <code>~/.config/opencode/opencode.json</code> (global)</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>Kiro</td>
<td><code>.kiro/settings/mcp.json</code> (project) or <code>~/.kiro/settings/mcp.json</code> (global)</td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></markdown-accessiblity-table>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Privacy</h2><a id="user-content-privacy" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Privacy" href="#privacy"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Argent collects opt-out usage and diagnostic telemetry to help us prioritise features and fix what breaks.</p>
<p dir="auto">You can opt out at any time:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="argent telemetry disable   # check status with: argent telemetry status"><pre>argent telemetry disable   <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> check status with: argent telemetry status</span></pre></div>
<p dir="auto">For the full details — see the <a href="https://github.com/software-mansion/argent/blob/main/Telemetry.md">Argent Privacy Notice (Telemetry)</a>.</p>

<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Argent is created by Software Mansion</h2><a id="user-content-argent-is-created-by-software-mansion" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Argent is created by Software Mansion" href="#argent-is-created-by-software-mansion"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Since 2012 <a href="https://swmansion.com" rel="nofollow">Software Mansion</a> is a software agency with experience in building web and mobile apps. We are Core React Native Contributors and experts in dealing with all kinds of React Native issues. We can help you build your next dream product – <a href="https://swmansion.com/contact/projects?utm_source=argent&amp;utm_medium=readme" rel="nofollow">Hire us</a>.</p>
